Motivation: 本文研究有限局部环 $R=\mathbb{F}_p[x]/\langle x^4\rangle$ 上的零因子图 $\Gamma(R)$ 的谱性质及其在网络分析中的应用。动机在于利用理想滤过结构深入理解这类代数图的谱分解特征，并探索其在网络可靠性、线性共识和随机游走等应用中的行为。

Method: 采用由理想滤过 $\langle u^3\rangle\subset\langle u^2\rangle\subset\langle u\rangle$ 诱导的公平4-胞腔划分，对零因子图进行谱分析。分别计算邻接谱、拉普拉斯谱，并基于此推导树熵、网络可靠性、线性共识动力学、基尔霍夫指数以及随机游走的渐近性质。

Result: 证明了邻接谱分解为离散水平 $\lambda\in\{0,-1\}$（重数分别为 $p^3-p^2-1$ 和 $p^2-3$）以及一个不可约三次多项式的三个无理根，邻接能量为 $\varepsilon(\Gamma(R))=4p^2+\mathcal{O}(p)$。拉普拉斯谱完全为整数谱。树熵、网络不可靠性、线性共识的模态时间尺度、代数连通度、$H_2$ 稳态误差方差、基尔霍夫指数及平均电阻距离、马尔可夫随机游走的渐近流量集中向量等均得到精确或渐近表达式。

Conclusion: 通过理想滤过诱导的胞腔划分，完整刻画了 $\mathbb{F}_p[x]/\langle x^4\rangle$ 零因子图的谱结构，揭示了其拉普拉斯谱的整数性和邻接谱的代数复杂性，并在网络动力学和随机过程方面获得了丰富的定量结果，展示了代数图论在网络科学中的深刻应用。

Abstract: For an odd prime $p$, let $Γ(R)$ denote the zero-divisor graph associated with the finite local ring $R= \mathbb{F}_p[x]/\langle x^4 \rangle$, having $p^3 - 1$ vertices and size $|E(Γ(R))| = \frac{1}{2}(3p^4 - 4p^3 - p^2 + 2)$. Using an equitable 4-cell partition induced by the ideal filtration $\langle u^3 \rangle \subset \langle u^2 \rangle \subset \langle u \rangle$, we prove that $\operatorname{Spec}_A(Γ(R))$ decomposes into discrete levels $λ\in \{0, -1\}$ with multiplicities $p^3 - p^2 - 1$ and $p^2 - 3$, respectively, alongside three irrational roots of an irreducible cubic polynomial $P_3(λ) = 0$, yielding adjacency energy $ε(Γ(R)) = 4p^2 + \mathcal{O}(p)$. In contrast, the Laplacian spectrum is entirely integral: $\operatorname{Spec}_L(Γ(R)) = \{0^1, (p - 1)^{p^3 - p^2}, (p^2 - 1)^{p^2 - p - 1}, (p^3 - 1)^{p - 1}\}$. Consequently, the tree entropy satisfies $z(Γ(R)) = \ln p - \frac{1}{p} + \frac{\ln p}{p} + \mathcal{O}(\frac{\ln p}{p^2})$, with small-failure unreliability bounded by $p^2(p - 1)(1 - q)^{p-1}$. In linear consensus $\dot{x} = -Lx$, error trajectories decouple into three modal timescales $\{ (p^3 - 1)^{-1}, (p^2 - 1)^{-1}, (p - 1)^{-1} \}$ with algebraic connectivity $α(Γ(R)) = p - 1$ and steady-state $H_2$ error variance $H_2^2 = \frac{Kf(Γ(R))}{2n^2} = \frac{1}{2p} + \mathcal{O}(p^{-3})$. Furthermore, the Kirchhoff index yields a vanishing average resistance distance $\overline{r}(Γ(R)) = \frac{2}{p} + \mathcal{O}(p^{-3}) \to 0$, while unbiased Markovian random walks induce an asymptotic traffic concentration vector $Π= (\frac{1}{3}, 0, \frac{1}{3}, \frac{1}{3})$ with quadratic per-node core routing stress $π_{\mathrm{core}}/π_{\mathrm{periph}} \sim p^2 + p + 1$.

</details>


### [82] [Wideband Physical Layer Security in Mixed Near-Field and Far-Field Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22936)
*Yunpu Zhang,Changsheng You,Hing Cheung So*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基站配备超大规模阵列时混合近远场宽带通信的物理层安全问题，发现各子载波具有不同的安全传输区域，并证明频率选择性人工噪声可将不安全子载波转为安全子载波；提出了两阶段混合波束成形算法及低复杂度AN设计方案，有效保障多用户在混合场条件下的安全通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宽带物理层安全（PLS）研究大多仅关注远场或近场通信系统，缺乏对混合近远场宽带通信场景的研究。实际中，配备超大规模阵列（XL-array）的基站可能在服务远场合法用户的同时，面临近场窃听者的近距离截获威胁，这种混合场场景更具一般性和实用性。

Method: 1. 建立优化问题：在满足每子载波功率约束下，联合设计发射波束成形和人工噪声（AN）以最大化所有子载波上所有合法用户的保密速率。2. 特殊案例分析：针对单合法用户和单窃听者场景，基于菲涅尔积分推导子载波特定的保密条件，分析混合场宽带系统中的安全传输区域特性。3. 多用户场景：提出两阶段混合波束成形算法联合设计发射波束成形和AN；进一步开发低复杂度算法，通过将AN波束成形器直接与窃听者信道对齐来消除跨所有子载波优化AN波束成形器的需求。

Result: 1. 理论揭示混合场宽带系统中各子载波的安全传输区域并不相同，这是由菲涅尔积分导出的子载波特定保密条件所决定的。2. 证明引入频率选择性AN的两个关键优势：可将原本不安全的子载波转变为安全子载波，并显著提升整体保密性能。3. 提出的两阶段混合波束成形算法和多用户低复杂度AN设计算法，能够在混合场条件下确保多用户安全传输。

Conclusion: 本文研究了混合近远场宽带通信系统中的物理层安全问题，揭示了混合场宽带PLS的独特特性（频率相关的安全传输区域），并通过理论分析和算法设计证明了频率选择性AN在提升保密性能方面的重要作用。所提出的低复杂度算法为实际大规模系统部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Prior studies on wideband physical layer security (PLS) have mostly focused on either far-field or near-field communication systems. In this paper, we investigate PLS in a more general and practical mixed near-field and far-field wideband communication scenario, where a base station (BS) equipped with an extremely large-scale array (XL-array) serves multiple legitimate users in the far field, while a near-field eavesdropper attempts to intercept confidential information at close range. Specifically, we formulate an optimization problem to maximize the secrecy rate of all legitimate users across all subcarriers by jointly designing the transmit beamforming and artificial noise (AN), subject to per-subcarrier power constraints. To gain useful insights into the unique characteristics of mixed-field wideband PLS, we first analyze a special case with a single legitimate user and an eavesdropper. Interestingly, we show that the secure transmission regions in mixed-field wideband systems are not the same across all subcarriers, as revealed by subcarrier-specific secrecy conditions derived from the Fresnel integrals. Moreover, we theoretically demonstrate that introducing frequency-selective AN into the system offers two key benefits: 1) it transforms originally insecure subcarriers into secure ones, and 2) it substantially improves the overall secrecy performance. For the general multiuser setup, we propose an efficient two-stage hybrid beamforming algorithm to jointly design the transmit beamforming and AN, ensuring secure transmission under mixed-field conditions. Building on our analytical insights, we further develop an efficient and low-complexity algorithm that designs the AN beamformers by directly aligning them with the eavesdropper's channel, thereby eliminating the need to optimize AN beamformers across all subcarriers.

</details>


### [83] [Hamiltonian Monte Carlo for Vector Perturbation Precoding in MU-MIMO via Continuous Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23011)
*Junichiro Hagiwara,Toshihiko Nishimura,Yasutaka Ogawa,Takeo Ohgane*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）的连续松弛方法，将MU-MIMO下行预编码中向量扰动（VP）的离散整数扰动向量搜索问题转化为连续t分布混合空间中的概率推断问题，将搜索复杂度从指数级降低至O(N^2)，在10^-3误码率下与VP性能极限的差距仅为2.4 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户MIMO（MU-MIMO）下行预编码中，向量扰动（VP）作为一种代表性非线性方法虽能实现高性能，但其整数扰动向量的搜索等价于最近向量问题（CVP），随着用户数增加，计算复杂度急剧增长，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 将整数扰动的离散结构松弛为连续t分布混合模型，利用基于梯度的哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）方法进行高效采样搜索，将原离散优化问题重新表述为连续空间中的概率推断问题。

Result: 所提方法的搜索复杂度随用户数N呈O(N^2)多项式缩放；数值实验表明，在符号误码率10^-3处，其性能与逼近VP性能极限的超球近似基准相差仅2.4 dB。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将VP扰动搜索重构为概率推断问题，为高维离散搜索在连续空间中的处理提供了通用框架，在保持接近最优性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) is a key technology that improves wireless capacity through multiple antennas. In MU-MIMO downlink precoding, vector perturbation (VP) is a representative nonlinear method that achieves high performance. However, its search for the integer perturbation vector reduces to a closest vector problem, whose complexity grows rapidly as the number of users increases. We propose a method that relaxes the discrete structure of the integer perturbation into a continuous mixture of $t$-distributions, enabling efficient search via gradient-based Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC). Complexity analysis and numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Its search complexity scales as O(N^2) in the number of users N. At a symbol error rate of 10^-3, it performs within 2.4 dB of a hypersphere approximation benchmark, which approximates the performance limit of VP. This paper reframes the VP perturbation search as a probabilistic inference problem, providing a general formulation for handling high-dimensional discrete search in a continuous space.

</details>


### [84] [Black-Box Attack for IRS-Aided Communications with BER-Only Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23112)
*Zhengkai Tu,Jimmy Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出PRIS算法，在仅能获得用户BER反馈的黑盒条件下，通过渐进式搜索和优先回放机制学习有效IRS相位配置，以最大化多用户中最小BER，实现对IRS辅助MISO下行链路的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能反射面（IRS）是一种增强合法通信的有前景的无线技术，但存在安全漏洞：攻击者可以重新配置IRS来降低合法数据传输质量。现有研究多假设攻击者拥有信道信息或符号级观测，而实际中攻击者可能仅能获取用户报告的误码率（BER），这种黑盒攻击场景尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出PRIS（Progressive IRS attack）方法，一种在线搜索算法：1）从大规模块修改到单元素精化的渐进式搜索策略；2）利用退火用户均衡奖励函数处理噪声BER反馈；3）采用优先经验回放机制加速学习有效相位转移模式；4）在有限非重复测试预算约束下优化IRS配置。

Result: 数值结果表明，PRIS在最小化BER指标上优于现有基准方法，能够在仅依赖BER反馈的黑盒设置下有效识别恶化合法通信的IRS配置。

Conclusion: PRIS通过渐进式搜索和噪声感知学习机制，解决了仅基于BER反馈的IRS黑盒攻击问题，为IRS辅助通信系统的安全防御设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Intelligent reflecting surface (IRS) has emerged as a promising wireless technology for enhancing legitimate communications. In this paper, we investigate an IRS-assisted multiuser MISO downlink network in which an attacker reconfigures the IRS to degrade legitimate data transmission. Specifically, we consider a black-box attack setting in which the attacker has access to neither channel information nor symbol-level observations and can observe only the BER reported by each user. The objective of the attacker is to identify an IRS configuration that maximizes the minimum BER among all users under a limited budget of non-repeated tests. To address this BER-only black-box attack problem, we propose PRIS, an online search method that learns effective phase transitions from previous trials and progressively narrows the search from large-block modifications to single-element refinement. Specifically, an annealed user-balancing reward and prioritized replay are incorporated to exploit noisy BER feedback. Numerical results demonstrate that PRIS achieves a higher minimum BER than existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [85] [The Deterministic Broadcast Channel Revisited: A Bipartite Graph Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23322)
*Yiyu Qiu,Wei Chen,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文用二分图模型对确定性广播信道进行纯确定性组合分析，首次获得了一般情形的显式闭式容量区域（用度序列表示）和确定性容量可达编码方案，避免了传统方法的概率数值优化，并可通过二元线性规划实现最优有限块长编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播信道（BC）是网络/多用户信息理论中的核心问题，而确定性广播信道（det-BC）对理解一般广播信道至关重要。现有Marton和Pinsker在1970年代使用随机编码方法对det-BC的研究是概率性的，且容量区域表示为输出符号熵的函数。本文旨在提供一种纯确定性和组合化的处理方法，以获得更明确的容量区域刻画，避免对输入符号概率分布进行数值优化。

Method: 本文提出将每个det-BC唯一且完整地表征为一个二分图模型。基于该二分图模型，利用度序列（degree sequences）来显式表达容量区域边界上的每个点，边界坐标由切线斜率参数化。对于由多个孤立子图组成的二分图，研究公共可解码消息的存在性及其可达速率。最后，通过二元线性规划构造det-BC的最优有限块长编码。

Result: 1) 得到了一般det-BC的显式容量区域，每个边界点都表示为两部分度序列的显式函数；2) 提出了确定性的容量可达编码方案；3) 对于Blackwell信道等特殊情形之外的det-BC，首次获得了闭式容量区域，无需对输入符号概率分布进行数值优化；4) 对于多孤立子图构成的二分图，显式刻画了在保持最大可达和速率条件下公共可解码消息的可达速率；5) 通过二分图表述，利用二元线性规划实现了最优有限块长编码的构造。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入二分图模型，为确定性广播信道提供了一种纯确定性和组合化的分析方法，成功获得了显式闭式容量区域和确定性容量可达编码方案，克服了传统概率方法需要数值优化的局限性。该方法还揭示了多子图结构中公共消息传输的可能性，并为实际编码构造提供了基于二元线性规划的有效工具。

Abstract: The general broadcast channel (BC) has attracted considerable attention because it is a central problem in network or multi-user information theory. The deterministic BC (det-BC), in which both output symbols are functions of the input symbol, plays a vital role in understanding general BCs. In the 1970s, Marton and Pinsker investigated the det-BC independently using a random coding approach and hence quantified its capacity region as functions of the entropy of output symbols. In this paper, we present a purely deterministic and combinatorial treatment of the deterministic BC, which is in contrast to Marton and Pinsker's probabilistic treatment. In particular, we show that each det-BC can be uniquely and completely characterized by a bipartite graph. Based on the bipartite graph model, we present the explicit capacity region of the general det-BC and a deterministic capacity-achieving coding scheme. Specifically, each point on the capacity region boundary is expressed as an explicit function of the degree sequences of the two parts, with boundary coordinates parametrized by the tangent slope. As a result, a closed-form capacity region, which was previously unknown except for special cases such as the Blackwell channel, can be analytically determined, without optimizing the probability distribution of input symbols numerically. For a bipartite graph consisting of multiple isolated subgraphs, there can exist a commonly decodable message while preserving the maximum achievable sum rate, and its achievable rate is explicitly characterized. Finally, the bipartite-graph formulation allows us to construct optimal finite-blocklength coding for the det-BC through binary linear programming.

</details>


### [86] [GNN-Based Global CSI Reconstruction for Fronthaul-Limited Distributed MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23365)
*Haojin Li,Kaiqian Qu,Anbang Zhang,Chen Sun,Wenqi Zhang,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）重建和任务驱动天线选择的分布式MIMO系统全局信道状态信息（CSI）获取框架，通过仅上传选定天线的CSI并在中央单元重建完整全局CSI，显著降低了前传开销，同时提升了CSI重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式多输入多输出（DMIMO）系统中，全局信道状态信息（CSI）获取对于协作预编码至关重要，但从所有分布式天线上传完整的瞬时CSI会产生沉重的前传开销，这限制了系统的可扩展性和实际部署效率。

Method: 本文提出了一种前传高效的CSI获取框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）基于通用掩码条件图神经网络（GNN）的CSI重建机制，该网络使用随机上传掩码进行训练，能够从部分观测中重建完整全局CSI；（2）任务驱动的天线选择策略，在有限前传预算下评估天线子集，选择最优部署掩码后对GNN进行微调。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架在降低前传开销和导频开销的同时，实现了 improved 的CSI重建精度，证明了该方法在DMIMO系统中进行高效全局CSI获取的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结合GNN重建能力与任务驱动的天线选择，本文提出的框架为DMIMO系统中的前传高效全局CSI获取提供了有效解决方案，在减少开销的同时保证了重建质量，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Global channel state information (CSI) acquisition is essential for cooperative precoding in distributed multiple-input multiple-output (DMIMO) systems, but uploading full instantaneous CSI from all distributed antennas creates heavy fronthaul overhead. This paper proposes a fronthaul-efficient acquisition framework based on graph neural network (GNN) reconstruction and task-driven antenna selection. Each transmission and reception point (TRP) uploads only selected antenna CSI, while the centralized unit (CU) reconstructs the full global CSI from partial observations. A universal mask-conditioned GNN is trained with random upload masks, used to evaluate antenna subsets under a fronthaul budget, and then fine-tuned for the selected deployment mask. Simulation results show improved CSI reconstruction accuracy with lower fronthaul and pilot overhead.

</details>


### [87] [Online Wideband MIMO Channel Reconstruction from Periodically Swept RBs via Incremental CP Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23388)
*Boxin Huang,Libin Zheng,Minru Bai,Yuhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线规范多元分解追踪器（CP-PBCD），通过近端块更新在每个窄带资源块采集后重建完整宽带信道，以固定每帧计算预算实现亚毫秒级在线更新，在所有测试条件下均优于六种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性资源块扫描导致大部分宽带信道未被观测，且混合了不同年龄的测量值，需要一种能够实时重建完整信道的方法。

Method: 开发了基于近端块更新的在线规范多元分解追踪器（CP-PBCD），采用年龄加权有限历史、频率和时间正则化、有界分量管理来维持自适应低秩表示；使用两次热启动共轭梯度迭代和并行移位频率求解提供固定每帧更新预算；通过训练用户空间投影增强低导频信噪比下的噪声抑制。

Result: 在10个测试用户、3种速度、4种信噪比、1000帧完整轨迹（1毫秒RB采集）的实验中，直接首RB CP-PBCD在所有12种条件下均优于6种基线方法，获得更低的归一化均方误差（NMSE）；在3.6 km/h和20 dB条件下，比周期性物理重拟合改善3.91 dB，运行速度快35.1倍，平均每次在线更新仅需0.717毫秒；可选的分散启动导频改善早期采集，受控初始化研究在导频预算变化下最后100帧NMSE范围为0.427 dB。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过增量更新和亚毫秒级平均计算，可以实现精确的当前信道重建，为实时宽带信道估计提供了一种高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Periodic resource-block (RB) scanning leaves most of the current wideband channel unobserved and mixes measurements of different ages. We develop an online canonical polyadic tracker with proximal block updates (CP-PBCD) that reconstructs the full channel after each narrow-RB acquisition. Age-weighted finite histories, frequency and temporal regularization, and bounded component management maintain an adaptive low-rank representation. Two warm-started conjugate-gradient iterations and parallel shifted frequency solves provide a fixed per-frame update budget. Training-user spatial projection strengthens noise suppression at low pilot SNR. Experiments cover ten test users, three speeds, four SNRs, and complete 1000-frame trajectories with 1-ms RB acquisition. Direct first-RB CP-PBCD achieves lower mean normalized mean squared error (NMSE) than all six baselines in all twelve conditions. At 3.6 km/h and 20 dB, it improves on periodic physical refitting by 3.91 dB and runs 35.1 times faster, averaging 0.717 ms per online update. Optional dispersed startup pilots improve early acquisition, while a controlled initialization study yields a last-100-frame NMSE range of 0.427 dB across pilot budgets. These results demonstrate accurate current-channel reconstruction through incremental updates with submillisecond average computation.

</details>


### [88] [Remote IoT Source Monitoring with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23678)
*Andrea Munari*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对延迟反馈的有损信道IoT监控场景，提出并分析了反应式和主动式阈值报告策略，证明主动传输在严格速率约束下仍能显著降低估计均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程源监控是物联网（IoT）应用的关键用例，需要在具有延迟反馈的相关有损信道上实现高效通信协议以确保及时数据传输。现有研究缺乏针对实际IoT场景的实用报告策略分析，特别是在信道相关、存在反馈延迟的情况下的性能权衡研究。

Method: 本文研究基于阈值的实用报告策略来监控马尔可夫过程，评估两种方法：（1）反应式策略——节点在更新后等待反馈；（2）主动式策略——抢先传输第二个更新。利用马尔可夫更新过程（Markov renewal processes），对均方误差（MSE）进行精确性能分析。

Result: 研究刻画了估计误差与通信速率之间的基本权衡关系，结果表明：即使在严格的IoT速率约束下，主动传输也能显著改善MSE性能。

Conclusion: 在具有延迟反馈的相关有损信道中，主动式阈值报告策略相比反应式策略能显著提升远程监控的估计精度，为资源受限的IoT系统提供了有效的设计指导。

Abstract: Remote source monitoring is a key use case for Internet of things (IoT) applications, calling on efficient communications protocols to ensure timely data delivery. In this paper, we consider a practically inspired scenario in which an IoT device reports sensor readings to a gateway over a correlated, lossy channel, receiving feedback on the transmission outcome only after some delay. For this setting, we investigate practical threshold-based reporting strategies for monitoring a Markov process, and evaluate two approaches: a reactive strategy where the node awaits feedback after an update, and a proactive strategy that preemptively transmits a second update. Leveraging Markov renewal processes, we provide an exact performance analysis in terms of the mean squared error (MSE). Our study characterizes the fundamental trade-off between estimation error and communication rate, revealing that proactive transmissions significantly improve MSE even under strict IoT rate constraints.

</details>


### [89] [Joint Antenna Geometry and Transmit Covariance Design for Near-Field Multicast ISAC with Pinching Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23693)
*Hui Yang,Hao Feng,Ebrahim Bedeer,Ming Zeng,Mengyao Wang,Gaojian Huang,Mengyan Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由有损多波导PASS赋能的近场多播ISAC系统，联合优化PA位置和发射协方差矩阵以在满足多播通信约束下最小化目标参数估计的CRB，并提出了基于交替优化的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对基于波导的PASS架构在近场ISAC场景下的系统分析，特别是如何联合利用可重构的传播环境和几何依赖的辐射孔径来平衡通信与感知性能，以及波导损耗对系统设计的根本性影响。

Method: 1) 建立考虑波导衰减、等辐射功率操作、几何依赖自由空间传播和单基地感知的近场多播ISAC信号模型；2) 推导目标参数估计的FIM，利用块对角PA传输结构获得紧凑的投影雅可比表示；3) 基于该结构表征每波导和跨波导FIM贡献、损耗-孔径权衡、可辨识性条件和通信-感知相位冲突；4) 构建联合PA位置和发射协方差优化问题，设计交替优化算法：协方差子问题转化为半定规划求解，PA位置子问题采用波导级块坐标下降处理。

Result: 获得了FIM的投影雅可比紧凑表示，揭示了PA几何与发射协方差对CRB的联合影响机制；表征了有损多波导PASS的关键性能权衡与设计约束；提出的交替优化算法能够有效求解联合设计问题，实现多播通信约束下的感知精度最优化。

Conclusion: 有损多波导PASS为近场多播ISAC提供了灵活的架构基础，通过联合优化PA位置和发射协方差，可以在满足通信需求的同时显著提升感知性能。投影雅可比表示为理解PASS-ISAC系统的内在结构特性提供了理论工具，所提算法为实际系统设计提供了可扩展的优化框架。

Abstract: PASS provide a flexible waveguide-based architecture for reconfiguring wireless propagation environments and creating geometry-dependent radiating apertures. This paper investigates a near-field multicast ISAC system enabled by a lossy multi-waveguide PASS, where a base station transmits a common message to multiple communication users while simultaneously sensing one or multiple targets. The PA positions along the waveguides and the feed-domain transmit covariance matrix are jointly designed to improve sensing accuracy under multicast communication constraints. We first develop a near-field multicast ISAC signal model that accounts for waveguide attenuation, equal-radiated-power operation, geometry-dependent free-space propagation, and monostatic sensing. Then, we derive the FIM for target parameter estimation and obtain a compact projected-Jacobian representation by exploiting the block-diagonal PA transfer structure. This representation reveals how the PA geometry and transmit covariance jointly affect the CRB. Based on this structure, we further characterize the per-waveguide and cross-waveguide FIM contributions, the loss-aperture tradeoff, the identifiability condition, and the communication-sensing phase conflict. To minimize the CRB, we formulate a joint PA-position and transmit-covariance optimization problem subject to a multicast rate constraint, a feed-power budget, and PA deployment constraints. An alternating optimization algorithm is developed, where the covariance subproblem is solved as a semidefinite program and the PA-position subproblem is handled by waveguide-wise block coordinate descent.

</details>


### [90] [NOMA-Assisted Multi-User Hybrid Wireless-Fed Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23698)
*Hui Yang,Peng Zhu,Ming Zeng,Ebrahim Bedeer,Saeid Pakravan,Yulei Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出NOMA辅助的无线馈电夹持天线系统（Wi-PASS），通过联合优化功率分配、解码顺序和夹持天线位置，在28 GHz毫米波频段实现多用户高能效通信，相比传统OMA方案节省约20%功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文研究非正交多址（NOMA）辅助的多用户无线馈电夹持天线系统（Wi-PASS），旨在解决传统无线通信系统中多用户服务时的功率效率问题。通过引入夹持天线技术和NOMA机制，探索在毫米波频段（28 GHz）下如何有效降低系统总功耗，同时克服自干扰和信道衰落等挑战。

Method: 本文提出了一种NOMA辅助的Wi-PASS架构，其中多天线基站同时服务一个直连用户，并通过全双工放大转发中继无线馈电。中继将NOMA波形注入介质波导，一个位置可调的夹持天线服务两个额外用户。在最大增益迫零传输、理想连续干扰消除和加性残余自干扰模型下，通过联合优化基站功率、中继放大因子、NOMA功率系数、解码顺序和夹持天线位置来最小化总消耗功率。对于固定位置和解码顺序，通过变量变换将资源分配问题转化为严格凸的标量问题，得到闭式全局解。位置相关的解码顺序将波导划分为有限个区间，每个区间上优化功率的导数由二次多项式控制，从而通过评估小规模有限候选集找到全局最优位置。

Result: 在28 GHz频段、1000个随机用户拓扑的仿真结果表明，所提出的架构在直接传输、阵列馈电、无PASS和等时分正交多址（OMA）基准方案中始终需要最低消耗功率。在目标信干噪比为20、25和30 dB时，相对于等时OMA，平均功率分别降低19.2%、21.1%和21.7%。该优势在基站-中继距离和残余自干扰增加时仍然保持。

Conclusion: 本文提出的NOMA辅助Wi-PASS架构通过联合优化功率分配、解码顺序和夹持天线位置，实现了显著功率节省。夹持天线的位置可调性为系统提供了额外的自由度，使得在复杂信道条件下仍能保持能效优势。该研究为毫米波频段高能效多用户通信系统的设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates a non-orthogonal multiple-access (NOMA)-assisted multi-user wireless-fed pinching-antenna system (Wi-PASS). A multi-antenna base station (BS) simultaneously serves one direct user and wirelessly feeds a full-duplex amplify-and-forward relay equipped with a directional horn receiver. The relay injects the NOMA waveform into a dielectric waveguide, and one position-adjustable pinching antenna serves two additional users. Under maximum-gain zero-forcing transmission, ideal successive interference cancellation, and an additive residual self-interference model, we minimize the total consumed power by jointly optimizing the BS powers, relay amplification factor, NOMA power coefficients, decoding order, and pinching-antenna position. For a fixed position and decoding order, a variable transformation reduces the resource-allocation problem to a strictly convex scalar problem and yields a closed-form global solution. The position-dependent decoding order partitions the waveguide into finitely many intervals, and the derivative of the optimized power is governed by a quadratic polynomial on each interval. Hence, the globally optimal position is found by evaluating a small finite candidate set. Simulations at 28~GHz over 1000 random user topologies show that the proposed architecture consistently requires the lowest consumed power among direct-transmission, array-fed, no-PASS, and equal-time orthogonal multiple-access (OMA) benchmarks. At target signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratios of 20, 25, and 30~dB, it reduces the average power by 19.2%, 21.1%, and 21.7%, respectively, relative to equal-time OMA, while preserving its advantage as the BS-relay distance and residual SI increase.

</details>


### [91] [Envelopes of upper bounds for nonbinary constant-weight and constant-composition codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23869)
*Artur Akhiiarov,Peter Boyvalenkov,Danila Cherkashin,Andrei Raigorodskii*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的最优传输框架，将经典的Bassalygo-Elias和Levenshtein界推广到二元及非二元定重码，用互信息刻画组合间上界传递的渐近代价，证明定重速率函数的幺模性和Schur凹性，并通过整合现有界获得改进的编码理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从现有界推导码长上界是编码理论中的经典方法，源于Elias、Bassalygo和Levenshtein的开创性结果。本文旨在建立一个统一框架，涵盖Bassalygo-Elias和Levenshtein不等式在二元及非二元（定重）码中的应用，并提供某些推广。

Method: 1. 建立包含Bassalygo-Elias和Levenshtein不等式的统一框架；2. 用互信息表示不同符号组合之间传递界的渐近代价，建立信息论最优传输公式；3. 确定任意组合之间最优置换传输代价（用majorization序中的最小公共上界表示）；4. 研究对称定重组合的特殊情形；5. 证明闭包算子的幂等性以及传输与外Bassalygo-Elias平均化的交换性质；6. 建立上界成为闭包算子不动点的充要条件。

Result: 1. 得到任意组合间最优置换传输代价的显式表达式；2. 对称定重组合情形下获得显式传输轮廓；3. 证明渐近定重速率作为相对权重函数的幺模性；4. 证明闭包算子的幂等性；5. 证明先传输后外Bassalygo-Elias平均化不改变所得无限制界；6. 建立上界为闭包算子不动点的充要条件；7. 证明定组合速率函数的Schur凹性；8. 将现有上界整合为传输框架内的优化包络，获得改进的理论和数值界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的信息论最优传输框架，将经典的Bassalygo-Elias和Levenshtein不等式推广到更一般的情形。通过互信息和majorization理论，系统地研究了不同符号组合之间上界传递的渐近行为，揭示了定重/定组合码速率函数的深刻性质（幺模性、Schur凹性），并将现有结果整合为优化包络以获得更紧的界。

Abstract: Deriving upper bounds on code size from existing bounds is a classical approach in coding theory, dating back to the seminal results of Elias, Bassalygo, and Levenshtein. We study a framework encompassing the Bassalygo--Elias and Levenshtein inequalities for binary and nonbinary (constant-weight) codes and provides certain generalizations. The asymptotic cost of transferring a bound between different symbol compositions is expressed in terms of mutual information, yielding an information-theoretic optimal transport formulation.
  We determine the optimal permutation-transport cost between arbitrary compositions in terms of their least common majorant in the majorization order. Specializing to symmetric constant-weight compositions yields explicit transport profiles. As a byproduct, we establish unimodality of the asymptotic constant-weight rate as a function of the relative weight. We prove that the resulting closure operators are idempotent and that applying transport before outer Bassalygo--Elias averaging leaves the unrestricted bound obtained from the same input unchanged. We also establish necessary and sufficient conditions for an upper bound to be a fixed point of the closure operator. Since the asymptotic rate function is an upper bound for itself and it is a fixed point, we conclude the Schur concavity of the constant-composition rate function.
  Finally, we survey existing upper bounds for binary and nonbinary constant-weight and constant-composition codes, combine them into optimized envelopes within the transport framework, and obtain improved theoretical and numerical bounds.

</details>


### [92] [Communication-Efficient Distributed Training via Ring-Based Coded Approximate All-Reduce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23898)
*Sifat Munim,Aditya Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: CERAR是一种近似梯度聚合协议，通过将梯度分割为$c$个分量并利用线性编解码，将Ring All-Reduce的通信率从$2(N-1)/N$降低至$1+(N-2)/c$，同时以可控的数值稳定性代价实现任意小的近似误差，在低带宽多GPU集群中展现出显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模分布式训练中，Ring All-Reduce被广泛用于精确计算聚合梯度，但其归一化每工作节点通信量为$2(N-1)/N$。当工作节点数$N$较大时，通信开销成为瓶颈。因此，需要设计通信高效的近似梯度聚合协议，以降低通信成本。

Method: 提出了通信高效Ring All-Reduce（CERAR）协议，用于近似梯度聚合。核心方法包括：（1）将每个局部梯度分割为$c$个分量；（2）基于线性编解码操作，在$N$个工作节点的环拓扑上执行$L=c+N-2$轮通信；（3）提出显式的Vandermonde矩阵构造，使近似误差任意接近零；（4）提出乘法扰动构造，将条件数控制在$O(\epsilon^{-r_\star})$，其中$r_\star=\lceil c/N\rceil-1$；（5）建立条件数约束的优化问题，在近似误差和数值稳定性之间进行权衡。

Result: CERAR的归一化通信率为$1+(N-2)/c$，归一化存储为$1+N/c$。当$c$增大时，通信率和存储率均趋近于1。乘法扰动构造的误差为$O(\epsilon)$。在多GPU集群实验（低带宽和高带宽互连）中，低带宽环境下即使对于中等参数长度也显示出明显优势；预期在高带宽环境下，对于更高参数长度的实验也会有相应改进。

Conclusion: CERAR通过线性编解码和梯度分量分割，在近似梯度聚合中实现了接近最优的通信效率。虽然Vandermonde构造存在数值稳定性问题，但通过乘法扰动和条件数约束优化可以获得实用的稳定设计。实验验证了该方法在低带宽分布式训练场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Ring All-Reduce is widely used within large-scale distributed training for exact computation of the aggregate gradient. For a system with $N$ workers, its normalized per-worker communication is $2(N-1)/N$. In this work we present a communication-efficient Ring All-Reduce (CERAR) protocol for ``approximate'' gradient aggregation. CERAR partitions each local gradient into $c$ components and crucially relies on linear encoding and decoding operations. It performs $L=c+N-2$ communication rounds over an $N$-worker ring, yielding normalized communication rate $1+(N-2)/c$ and normalized storage $1+N/c$. We present an explicit Vandermonde-based construction, whose approximation error can be made arbitrarily close to zero with communication and storage rates approaching one with increasing $c$. However, this limit is achieved through ill-conditioned encoding matrices. Accordingly, we give a multiplicative perturbation construction whose error is $O(ε)$, while the relevant condition numbers are $O(ε^{-r_\star})$, where $r_\star=\lceil c/N\rceil-1$. This naturally motivates a condition-number-constrained optimization formulation for trading off the competing objectives and obtaining numerically stable practical designs. We present experiments on multi-GPU clusters with low-bandwidth and high-bandwidth interconnects. Our results demonstrate clear benefits in the low-bandwidth setting, even for moderate parameter length. We expect corresponding improvements even in the high-bandwidth setting for experiments with much higher parameter lengths.

</details>


### [93] [Exact Common Information and Exact Channel Synthesis for Correlated Discrete Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24120)
*Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文首次给出相关离散信源精确公共信息及精确信道合成速率区域的单字母表征，解决了精确信息理论中的两个核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决两个相关问题：一是为一对相关离散信源的精确公共信息（exact common information）提供单字母表征；二是为精确信道合成（exact channel synthesis）中共享随机速率与通信速率的可行域给出单字母表征。这些问题在信息论中涉及随机变量相关性的结构刻画，以及有限码长下精确模拟目标分布的理论极限。

Method: 作者采用单字母表征（single-letter characterization）的方法，即利用信息论中的基本量（如熵、互信息等）在单次字母表层面上的表达式来描述上述问题的最优解，而非多字母或极限形式。这种方法通常涉及随机变量辅助变量的引入，以及对精确（而非近似）合成条件的严格数学刻画。

Result: 论文成功给出了：1）一对相关离散信源精确公共信息的单字母表达式；2）精确信道合成中共享随机速率与通信速率的可行域的完整单字母表征。这些结果为精确信息理论中的基本问题提供了闭合形式的理论解答。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了精确信息理论（exact information theory）的框架，将Wyner公共信息等经典概念推广到精确情形，并为精确信道合成提供了速率区域的完整刻画，对分布式信源编码、信道模拟及密码学中的随机性提取等领域具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a single-letter characterization of the exact common information for a pair of correlated discrete sources, as well as a single-letter characterization of the admissible region for the shared randomness rate and the communication rate in exact channel synthesis.

</details>


### [94] [Dictators are most informative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24184)
*Vu Khac Ky,Tuan Tran*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 证明了Courtade-Kumar猜想：在独立二元噪声下，独裁者函数是所有布尔函数中保留输入信息最多的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔函数在噪声信道下的信息保持问题是一个重要的信息论和理论计算机科学问题。Courtade和Kumar曾提出一个猜想：在所有布尔函数中，独裁者函数（dictator function）在经过独立二元噪声信道后保留关于原始输入的信息量最大。证明这一猜想有助于深入理解噪声环境下信息处理的极限。

Method: 本文通过严格的数学证明来验证Courtade-Kumar猜想。具体技术细节在摘要中未详述，但涉及信息论工具、布尔函数分析以及可能的对称化或极值原理。

Result: 成功证明了Courtade-Kumar猜想：对于所有从{-1,1}^n到{-1,1}的布尔函数，在经过独立二元对称噪声信道后，独裁者函数（即f(x)=x_i对某个坐标i）保留关于均匀随机输入的信息量最大。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了独裁者函数在噪声信道信息保持方面的最优性，为布尔函数分析、信息论和计算复杂性理论提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove the Courtade-Kumar conjecture: among all Boolean functions $f\colon \{-1,1\}^n\to\{-1,1\}$, a dictator retains the most information about a uniformly random input observed through independent binary noise.

</details>


### [95] [High-Rate Quasi-Dyadic Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24201)
*Alessio Baldelli,Sisi Miao,Laurent Schmalen,Massimo Battaglioni,Marco Baldi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 基于准双对角矩阵设计高码率CSS QLDPC码，Tanner图围长≥6，有限长度逻辑错误率性能达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验码（QLDPC）是量子纠错编码的重要方向，但现有高码率CSS量子LDPC码的设计仍面临挑战。基于准双对角（quasi-dyadic）矩阵构造高性能QLDPC码是一个值得探索的方向，需要解决Tanner图围长（girth）控制和短环消除等关键问题。

Method: 提出一种基于准双对角矩阵的高码率Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS)量子LDPC码的新颖设计方案。通过构造两个分量经典码来构建CSS码，确保其Tanner图的围长至少为6。同时推导了校验矩阵四进制表示中长度为4的环的精确计数公式。

Result: 所提出的CSS QLDPC码在Tanner图结构上具有围长≥6的良好特性。在码容量（code-capacity）和现象学（phenomenological）两种噪声模型下的蒙特卡洛仿真表明，所提码的有限长度逻辑错误率性能与现有最先进码相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于准双对角矩阵的构造方法能够有效设计高码率CSS QLDPC码，通过控制围长和精确计数短环，获得了具有优异有限长度性能的量子纠错码，为量子通信和量子计算中的纠错提供了实用的编码方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel design of high-rate Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes based on quasi-dyadic matrices. The Tanner graphs associated with the two component classical codes used to construct the proposed CSS codes are characterized by a girth of at least $6$. We also derive an exact count of length-$4$ cycles in the quaternary representation of the parity-check matrix. Monte Carlo simulations under code-capacity and phenomenological noise models show that the proposed codes have competitive finite-length logical error rate performance compared with state-of-the-art codes in both these settings.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal Ferrers Diagram Rank-Metric Codes: New Constructions, Diagram Combinations, and Applications to Constant-Dimension Subspace Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24239)
*Fang-Wei Fu,Xuan Gao,Sihem Mesnager,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种基于最大秩距离（MRD）码子码的最优FDRM码新构造，并通过递归构造解决了两个公开问题，包括Zhang等人（2019）和Etzion等人（2016）提出的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ferrers图秩度量码（FDRM码）是常维码构造的关键组成部分，其最优构造一直是编码理论中的重要研究课题。现有文献中存在多个关于最优FDRM码存在性的开放问题，需要新的构造方法来推进该领域的理论发展。

Method: 1. 第一种构造：基于系统化MRD码的新生成矩阵族，构造最优FDRM码；2. 第二种构造：利用系统化MRD码生成矩阵的结构性质，在Ferrers图最右侧δ-2列每列至少包含n-1个点的条件下构造最优FDRM码；3. 第三种构造：放宽第二种构造的条件，仅要求最右侧δ-2列每列至少包含n-r个点（其中r<κ，κ=n-δ+1）；4. 递归构造：利用Ferrers图的proper combinations概念，从小规模FDRM码递归构造大规模最优FDRM码族。

Result: 1. 对于q≥7，证明了[𝓕,7]_q FDRM码的最优性，其中𝓕=[1,2,3,4,8,8,8,8,8]，解决了Zhang等人（2019）提出的开放问题；2. 在较弱的条件下（列点数要求从n-1降至n-r）获得新的最优FDRM码；3. 对于n×n Ferrers图𝓕，证明了当n为偶数时[𝓕,n/2-1]_q FDRM码的最优性，解决了Etzion等人（2016）提出的开放问题；4. 通过递归构造产生了多个新的最优FDRM码族。

Conclusion: 本文通过深入挖掘系统化MRD码的代数结构，提出了三种创新的最优FDRM码构造方法，并发展了基于Ferrers图组合的递归技术。这些成果不仅解决了编码理论中的两个重要开放问题，而且显著扩展了最优FDRM码的已知存在范围，为常维码的构造提供了更丰富的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present three new constructions of optimal FDRM codes, all derived from subcodes of maximum rank-distance (MRD) codes. The first construction (Theorem~\ref{theo5}) is based on a new family of generator matrices for systematic MRD codes and yields several previously unknown optimal FDRM codes. In particular, for $q\geq 7$, it establishes the optimality of $[\mathcal{F},7]_q$ FDRM codes with $ \mathcal{F}=[1,2,3,4,8,8,8,8,8]$, thereby resolving an open problem posed by Zhang \emph{et al.} (Des. Codes Cryptogr., 87(1):107--121, 2019).
  Our second construction exploits structural properties of generator matrices of a family of systematic MRD codes to obtain new optimal FDRM codes whenever each of the rightmost $δ-2$ columns of the Ferrers diagram $\mathcal{F}$ contains at least $n-1$ dots. Building upon this approach, we further develop a third construction by substantially relaxing this requirement: it is sufficient to assume that each of the rightmost $δ-2$ columns of $\mathcal{F}$ contains at least $n-r$ dots, where $r<κ$ and $κ=n-δ+1$.
  Furthermore, by exploiting the notion of proper combinations of Ferrers diagrams, we develop several recursive constructions that produce large FDRM codes from smaller building blocks, yielding a number of new optimal families. In particular, for an $n\times n$ Ferrers diagram $\mathcal{F}$ with prescribed parameters, one of these constructions establishes the optimality of $[\mathcal{F},\frac{n}{2}-1]_q$ FDRM codes whenever $n$ is even, thereby settling an open problem posed by Etzion \emph{et al.} (IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 62(4):1616--1630, 2016).

</details>


### [97] [On the Information-Theoretic Limits of Latent-Space Watermarking Through Pretrained Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24377)
*Jinwan Jeon,Minju Lee,Sung Hoon Lim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于预训练生成器的隐空间水印技术，针对确定性渲染器建立了率-密钥内外界，刻画了容量区域；推广到高斯模型并分析了密钥资源的最优分配；最后针对生成式水印特有的"再生攻击"威胁，刻画了标量高斯模型下的单轮复合容量及多轮再生下的容量衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的普及，对AI生成内容进行水印标识变得至关重要。现有水印方法多关注频域或像素域，而本文聚焦于生成器固有的隐空间（latent space）水印，利用预训练生成器的确定性渲染过程嵌入信息。此外，生成式水印面临独特的鲁棒性威胁——攻击者可通过重新采样对同一内容生成新实例，从而削弱或消除水印，这一"再生攻击"尚未被充分理论化。

Method: 1. 建立隐空间水印的基础通信模型：水印编码器利用消息和密钥选择隐变量输入，通过预训练生成器（渲染器）产生输出，要求输出精确匹配目标条件分布；2. 对有限字母表情形，推导率-密钥（rate-key）内外界，分析编码与协调（coordination）需求；3. 当渲染器可逆（输出分布唯一确定隐分布）时，通过强化逆证明得到容量区域，并证明其等价于显式保持预训练隐分布的情形；4. 推广到联合高斯模型，识别充分统计量以分离水印承载信息与隐变量协调需求；5. 对向量高斯模型，刻画密钥资源在特征模态间的最优分配；6. 引入"再生攻击"鲁棒性维度：攻击者利用已知语义上下文重新生成样本，建立复合信道模型；7. 对语义上下文编码器已知但检测器未知的标量高斯模型，刻画单轮复合容量；8. 扩展至多轮规范再生攻击，分析水印容量的衰减规律。

Result: 1. 有限字母表情形：获得率-密钥内外界，可逆渲染器下容量区域完全刻画，且保持预训练隐分布不降低容量；2. 联合高斯模型：识别出同时捕获输出端水印信息与隐变量协调需求的充分统计量；3. 向量高斯模型：给出密钥资源在特征模态间最优分配的闭式解；4. 标量高斯再生攻击模型（语义上下文编码器已知、检测器未知）：完整刻画单轮复合容量；5. 多轮再生攻击：刻画水印容量随再生轮次的衰减规律，揭示生成式水印在持续攻击下的理论极限。

Conclusion: 本文从信息论角度系统建立了隐空间生成水印的理论框架，涵盖基础容量刻画、高斯模型扩展及生成式特有的再生攻击分析。核心结论包括：隐空间水印的容量受渲染器可逆性、密钥资源分配及语义上下文信息非对称性共同约束；再生攻击构成生成式水印的本质威胁，其容量衰减规律为设计抗再生水印方案提供了理论基准。未来可进一步研究检测器未知上下文的更一般情形、非高斯渲染器及自适应攻击策略下的容量界。

Abstract: We study latent-space watermarking through a pretrained generator using a prescribed latent-to-output stochastic mapping, called the renderer. A watermark encoder selects the latent input using a message and secret key. For every message and semantic context, the released output must have exactly the desired conditional output distribution. For finite alphabets, we derive rate--key inner and outer bounds and characterize the coding and coordination requirements for realizing watermark communication through the prescribed latent interface. When the target output distribution of the generator uniquely determines the corresponding latent input distribution through the renderer, a strengthened converse yields the capacity region; the same region governs explicit preservation of the pretrained latent distribution. We extend the analysis to general jointly Gaussian models and identify a sufficient statistic of the latent that captures both the watermark-bearing information available at the generated output and the latent coordination required to preserve its target distribution. For the vector Gaussian model, we further characterize the optimal allocation of the secret-key resource across the resulting modes. Finally, we turn to an emerging robustness threat that is particularly natural in generative watermarking: an adversary can regenerate the released sample to obtain a fresh realization of the same underlying content while attenuating or destroying the embedded watermark. We incorporate this robustness axis into our framework and characterize the one-pass compound capacity of the scalar Gaussian model when the semantic context is known to the encoder but hidden from the detector, while the regeneration attack may depend on that context. Extending the analysis to multiple rounds of repeated canonical regeneration, we characterize the resulting watermark-capacity decay.

</details>


### [98] [Threshold Saturation from a Bounded Region in Multidimensional Spatially Coupled Codes over the BEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24568)
*Kenta Kasai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有界缩短区域可以在多维空间耦合规则LDPC和MacKay-Neal码上启动译码，在BEC信道下，MN码对多种度数选择均能达到信道容量，且缩短比例从V^{-1/d}改进到V^{-1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多维空间耦合LDPC和MN码在二进制擦除信道(BEC)上的译码启动问题，旨在降低缩短区域的比例并改善码率界限，同时探索在固定有限维度下耦合宽度与缩短区域大小独立于空间位置总数V的可能性。

Method: 1) 使用端点势恒等式(endpoint-potential identity); 2) 移除辅助约束条件; 3) 建立方向一致性的有限时间估计; 4) 利用曲率比较将平面边界进展转移到扩展球面上; 5) 对MN码，使用初等不等式证明所有相关度数的固定点正性; 6) 通过二维密度演化示例说明初始缩短区域的依赖性。

Result: 1) 证明有界缩短区域可在多维空间耦合规则LDPC和MN码上启动译码; 2) 均匀超立方耦合允许耦合宽度和缩短区域独立于空间位置总数V选择; 3) 在固定宽度和维度d>1时，将缩短板替换为有界超立方体使缩短比例从V^{-1/d}降至V^{-1}，校验数率界限中的缩短项获得相同改进; 4) 规则LDPC码在其非耦合势阈值以下译码; 5) MN码对所有整数度数选择ℓ>r≥2, g≥2达到容量：实际传输速率趋于r/ℓ，在和积译码下平均比特擦除概率在1-r/ℓ以下趋于零。

Conclusion: 本文通过结合势函数分析、几何方法和概率工具，严格证明了多维空间耦合码在BEC上的优越译码性能，特别是有界缩短区域可显著降低缩短开销，MN码在广泛参数范围内达到容量，为空间耦合码的理论分析和实际设计提供了重要理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that a bounded shortened region initiates decoding throughout multidimensional spatially coupled regular LDPC and MacKay--Neal (MN) codes over the binary erasure channel (BEC). In every fixed finite dimension, uniform hypercube coupling permits the coupling width and shortened region to be chosen independently of the total number $V$ of spatial positions. At fixed widths and dimension $d>1$, replacing a shortened slab by a bounded hypercube reduces the shortening fraction from order $V^{-1/d}$ to order $V^{-1}$, with the same improvement in the shortening term of the check-count rate bound. Regular LDPC codes decode below their uncoupled potential threshold. MN codes achieve capacity for every integer degree choice $\ell>r\geq2$, $g\geq2$: their actual transmitted rates tend to $r/\ell$ and their average bit-erasure probabilities under sum-product decoding vanish below $1-r/\ell$. The proof combines an endpoint-potential identity, removal of an auxiliary constraint, finite-time estimates uniform in direction, and a curvature comparison that transfers flat-boundary progress to expanding balls. For MN codes, elementary inequalities establish fixed-point positivity for all these degrees. Two-dimensional density-evolution examples illustrate the dependence on the initial shortened region; the general sufficient constants are not evaluated numerically.

</details>


### [99] [Single-Fragment Forensic Coding via Multidimensional Cyclically Permutable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24575)
*Yubo Sun,Yijun Zhang,Gennian Ge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对3D打印安全与取证问题，提出了一种单碎片取证编码方案。通过引入多维循环可置换码（CPCs），将取证对齐问题转化为多维循环同步问题，实现了从任意包含足够大轴平行盒的碎片中恢复唯一标识符的目标。作者给出了噪声和无噪声情况下的理论界与显式构造，在厚碎片条件下码率可趋近于1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D打印技术的普及带来了新的安全与取证挑战，特别是未经授权制造不可追踪枪支等管制物品的风险。现有技术缺乏有效的追溯机制，无法从打印物体的碎片中恢复标识信息。因此，需要一种能够将唯一标识符嵌入打印物体中，并能从任意足够大的碎片中恢复的编码方案，以实现物品的溯源追踪。

Method: 1. 提出单碎片取证编码问题：要求从包含最小体积和坐标厚度约束的轴平行盒的任意碎片中恢复唯一标识符，允许存在替换错误。
2. 引入多维循环可置换码（CPCs）：每个循环平移唯一确定原始码字和应用的平移量。
3. 采用周期提升技术：将CPC基数组进行周期性提升，使碎片中的每个完整周期对应于码字的一个未知循环平移。
4. 将取证对齐问题转化为多维循环同步问题。
5. 提出鲁棒行锚构造法：针对二进制字母表和$d\geq 2$的情况，实现显式构造。

Result: 1. 噪声环境下最优冗余度：对于固定维度$d$和字母表大小$q$，无噪声$d$维CPC的最优冗余度为$d\log_q k + o(1)$。
2. 替换错误校正：对于固定的$t$个替换错误，最优冗余度在$(t+1)d\log_q k + O(1)$和$(2t+1)d\log_q k + O(1)$之间。
3. 二进制显式构造：对于二进制字母表和$d\geq 2$，鲁棒行锚构造达到$(t+1)d\log_2 k + O(\log\log k)$冗余度，匹配最优首项。
4. 厚碎片区域性能：当$h = cM^{1/d}$时，周期提升产生的单碎片取证码率为$c^d - o(1)$；特别地，当$c = 1 - o(1)$时，码率趋近于1。

Conclusion: 本文通过多维循环可置换码和周期提升技术，成功解决了3D打印物体的单碎片取证编码问题。理论分析和显式构造表明，该方法在噪声环境下具有接近最优的性能，尤其在厚碎片条件下可实现接近1的码率。这项工作为3D打印安全、物品溯源和数字取证提供了理论基础和技术方案，可应用于管制物品的追踪与识别。

Abstract: The proliferation of 3D printing raises new security and forensic challenges, including the risk of unauthorized fabrication of untraceable firearms and other regulated items. To enable traceability, we consider \emph{single-fragment forensic coding}, in which a unique identifier is embedded into a printed object and must be recoverable from any fragment containing an axis-parallel box of prescribed minimum volume and coordinatewise thickness, even in the presence of substitution errors. To address this problem, we introduce and study multidimensional cyclically permutable codes (CPCs), for which every cyclic translate uniquely determines both the original codeword and the applied translation. By applying periodic lifting to a CPC base array, every complete period contained in a fragment corresponds to an unknown cyclic translate of a codeword, thereby reducing the forensic alignment problem to multidimensional cyclic synchronization.
  We establish theoretical bounds and develop explicit constructions in both the noiseless and substitution-error settings. For fixed dimension $d$ and alphabet size $q$, we obtain the optimal redundancy $d\log_q k+o(1)$ for noiseless $d$-dimensional CPCs of side length $k$. For fixed $t$, the optimal redundancy of $t$-substitution-correcting $d$-dimensional CPCs lies between $(t+1)d\log_q k+O(1)$ and $(2t+1)d\log_q k+O(1)$. For binary alphabets and $d\geq2$, an explicit robust row-anchor construction achieves $(t+1)d\log_2 k+O(\log\log k)$ redundancy, matching the optimal leading term. In the thick-fragment regime $h=cM^{1/d}$, where $M$ and $h$ lower-bound the volume and the side lengths of a box contained in the fragment, periodic lifting yields single-fragment forensic codes of rate $c^d-o(1)$. In particular, the rate approaches one when $c=1-o(1)$.

</details>


### [100] [Deterministic Smoothed MAP Detection for High-Dimensional MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24592)
*Vitor Tucci Ramos,Stephane Senecal,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性平滑最大后验（SMAP）框架用于高维MIMO检测，通过高斯混合近似离散星座先验实现连续优化，并开发了硬判决（SMAP-KR）和软输出两种变体，在大规模MIMO系统中展现出优异的性能-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维MIMO检测是一个具有挑战性的组合优化问题。传统的基于采样的方法计算复杂度高，而现有确定性方法在高维场景下性能受限。需要一种能够在保持星座结构的同时实现连续优化，并且可以灵活地作为独立检测器或现有检测器后处理精化阶段的框架。

Method: 1. 核心框架：将离散星座先验近似为独立同分布高斯混合，构建可微分的MAP目标函数，实现确定性优化而非采样；
2. SMAP-KR硬检测：在连续解基础上进行局部K_R邻域搜索，使用原始最大似然准则评估；
3. 退火延续（Annealed continuation）：提升高阶星座的鲁棒性；
4. 双邻域近似：降低混合先验评估的计算成本；
5. 软输出SMAP：基于平滑后验曲率的局部高斯近似，直接计算符号概率和比特对数似然比，无需后验采样或显式反假设列表。

Result: 1. 在临界负载多用户MIMO系统中，SMAP-KR随着系统维度增大呈现出越来越有利的性能-复杂度权衡；
2. SMAP-KR能够有效精化现有检测器的输出；
3. 在编码传输中，软输出SMAP相比LMMSE和K-best软检测在误块率方面获得显著提升。

Conclusion: SMAP框架通过确定性平滑优化为高维MIMO检测提供了一种灵活高效的解决方案。其可初始化特性使其兼具独立检测器和精化模块的双重功能，硬判决和软输出两种模式分别适用于不同场景，在大规模系统中展现出优异的扩展性和性能优势。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic smoothed maximum a posteriori (SMAP) framework for high-dimensional multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection. The discrete constellation prior is approximated by an i.i.d. Gaussian mixture, yielding a differentiable MAP objective that retains the constellation structure while enabling continuous optimization. Unlike sampling-based approaches, SMAP formulates detection as a deterministic optimization problem and can be initialized by the output of an arbitrary detector, allowing it to operate either as a standalone detector or as a refinement stage. For hard detection, we introduce SMAP-KR, which complements the continuous solution with a local $K_R$-neighbor search evaluated according to the original maximum-likelihood metric. Annealed continuation improves robustness for higher-order constellations, while a two-neighbor approximation reduces the cost of evaluating the mixture prior. We further develop a soft-output SMAP method in which a local Gaussian approximation based on the curvature of the smoothed posterior provides symbol probabilities and bit log-likelihood ratios without posterior sampling or explicit counterhypothesis lists. Numerical results for critically loaded MU-MIMO systems show that SMAP-KR provides an increasingly favorable performance-complexity tradeoff as the system dimension grows and can also effectively refine the output of existing detectors. For coded transmission, soft SMAP provides substantial block-error-rate gains over both LMMSE- and K-best-based soft detection.

</details>


### [101] [The Value of Duplicate Measurements When Decoding Second-Order Reed-Muller Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24647)
*Ziang Gao,Robert Calderbank*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文比较了RPA与CHIRRUP两种解码算法在二阶Reed-Muller码上的性能，发现CHIRRUP在特定参数条件下通过更有效的重复测量利用和树搜索策略优于标准RPA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶Reed-Muller码的解码是编码理论中的重要问题。现有算法中，RPA算法通过恢复与斜对称矩阵关联的二元二次型进行解码，而CHIRRUP算法通过恢复与对称矩阵关联的Z4值二次型进行解码。需要系统比较这两种算法的性能差异，并理解其背后的数学机制，以指导算法选择和改进。

Method: 1) 利用Gray映射建立两种二次型赋值向量之间的联系，证明其诱导了斜对称矩阵M与对称矩阵P之间的保秩对应关系；2) 基于Delsarte-Goethals集合DG(m,r)，分析赋值向量间欧氏距离如何由矩阵秩差异控制；3) 比较两种算法对重复测量的利用效率；4) 分析RPA的核感知变体（通过在ker M的陪集上聚合投影方向来处理奇异二次型）与CHIRRUP的树搜索策略（组合多行估计）在不同参数条件下的性能。

Result: 1) Gray映射在两种二次型的赋值向量之间建立了保秩对应关系；2) Delsarte-Goethals集合DG(m,r)上，赋值向量的欧氏距离由矩阵秩差异决定；3) CHIRRUP比RPA更有效地利用重复测量；4) RPA的核感知变体通过聚合ker M陪集上的投影方向可改善奇异二次型上的性能；5) 当r < (m-1)/2时，CHIRRUP的树搜索在DG(m,r)上比标准RPA更高效地组合多行估计，性能更优。

Conclusion: CHIRRUP算法在Delsarte-Goethals集合DG(m,r)上当r < (m-1)/2时优于标准RPA算法，这归因于其更高效的重复测量利用和树搜索策略。RPA的核感知变体可改善奇异二次型上的性能，但CHIRRUP在特定参数范围内整体表现更佳。这些结果为二阶Reed-Muller码的解码算法选择提供了理论依据：在r较小的Delsarte-Goethals集合上优先选择CHIRRUP，同时核感知技术可作为RPA改进的方向。

Abstract: We compare the performance of the RPA and CHIRRUP decoding algorithms for second-order Reed--Muller codes. The RPA algorithm recovers a binary quadratic form associated with a skew-symmetric matrix $M$, whereas CHIRRUP recovers a $\mathbb{Z}_4$-valued quadratic form associated with a symmetric matrix $P$. We describe how the Gray map connects the evaluation vectors of these forms, inducing a rank-preserving correspondence between $M$ and $P$. We analyze how Euclidean distance between evaluation vectors is governed by matrix rank differences using Delsarte--Goethals sets $DG(m,r)$. We demonstrate that CHIRRUP uses repeated measurements more effectively than RPA on these ensembles. While a kernel-aware variant of RPA improves performance on singular quadratic forms by aggregating projection directions across cosets of $\ker M$, CHIRRUP's tree search combines multiple row estimates more efficiently on $DG(m,r)$ when $r < \frac{m-1}{2}$, outperforming vanilla RPA.

</details>


### [102] [Exact Second-Order Asymptotics in Covert Communication Over Discrete Memoryless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24675)
*Qiaosheng Zhang,Lin Zhou,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了二元输入离散无记忆信道下，以变分距离度量隐蔽性时的二阶渐近性能。先前工作的一阶渐近已解决，但二阶项的可达界与逆界存在 $n^{1/4}$ 阶间隙。作者通过Bhattacharyya系数和对数似然比连续函数的期望重新表达变分距离，结合特征函数分析和高斯平滑论证，将逼近误差从 $O(n^{-1/4})$ 改进至 $O(n^{-1/2})$，从而消除了间隙，首次确立了精确的二阶渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐蔽通信（covert communication）研究如何在不被检测器（warden）发现的前提下可靠传输信息。现有工作已解决一阶渐近问题，但在二阶渐近分析中，Tahmasbi和Bloch (2019) 的可达界与逆界之间存在一个 $n^{1/4}$ 阶的惩罚项，导致二阶项无法精确确定。消除这一间隙、建立精确的二阶渐近理论是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 1. 采用脉冲位置调制（Pulse-Position Modulation, PPM）作为编码方案；2. 将变分距离通过Bhattacharyya系数和两个分布的连续函数期望重新表达；3. 利用对数似然比（log-likelihood ratio）的连续函数期望替代传统的事件概率分析；4. 结合特征函数（characteristic function）分析方法；5. 引入高斯平滑论证（Gaussian smoothing argument）以控制逼近误差。

Result: 成功将变分距离的逼近误差从先前工作的 $O(n^{-1/4})$（基于Berry-Esseen界）改进至 $O(n^{-1/2})$；消除了 $n^{1/4}$ 阶的惩罚项，使可达界与现有逆界匹配；首次确立了二元输入离散无记忆信道下隐蔽通信的精确二阶渐近性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过更精细的数学分析工具，解决了隐蔽通信二阶渐近理论中的关键间隙问题。核心贡献在于证明：通过对warden输出分布的 sharper 分析，特别是利用Bhattacharyya系数和连续函数期望结合特征函数方法，可以显著提高逼近精度。这一技术突破不仅完成了精确二阶渐近刻画，也为相关领域（如假设检验、信息论渐近分析）提供了有价值的分析工具。

Abstract: We determine the exact second-order asymptotics of covert communication over binary-input discrete memoryless channels when covertness is measured by variational distance. Previous work by Tahmasbi and Bloch [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, Apr. 2019] characterized the first-order asymptotics and derived achievability and converse bounds on the second-order term, but these bounds do not match. The gap arises from an additional penalty of order \(n^{1/4}\) in the achievability bound. We show that this penalty can be removed through a sharper analysis of the distribution of the warden's output induced by pulse-position modulation. Specifically, we express the variational distance through the Bhattacharyya coefficient of two distributions and the expectation of a continuous function of the log-likelihood ratio. Because the resulting expectation involves a continuous function rather than the probability of a likelihood-ratio event, an analysis of the characteristic function combined with a Gaussian smoothing argument reduces the approximation error from \(O(n^{-1/4})\) (derived from the Berry--Esseen bound in prior work) to \(O(n^{-1/2})\). With this better controlled approximation error, we manage to derive a matching achievability result to the existing converse result, thus establishing the exact second-order asymptotics.

</details>


### [103] [Orbit Reduction and Learned Run Distributions for Finite-Blocklength Binary Deletion Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24889)
*Hassan Khodaiemehr,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对二进制删除信道有限块长容量C_N(d)，本文利用对称性将优化简化为轨道权重，提出优化游程分布(ORD)模型及混合神经-精确求解方法，并通过"ORD连续迁移"将小规模精确解扩展到N=512，在中等删除概率下优于平坦游程分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于二进制删除信道，现有研究主要关注无限块长容量极限C(d)，但实际系统中更关键的是有限块长容量C_N(d)。然而，有限块长情况下的优化问题极其复杂，需要寻找有效的结构化输入分布来降低优化维度。

Method: 1. 利用对称性简化：证明最优输入分布在补集轨道和排列等价轨道上可以是常数，将优化问题简化为每个轨道一个权重；2. 提出优化游程分布(ORD)：一种N参数游程计数模型，在N≤3时与C_N(d)精确吻合，在所有游程计数恒定输入中最优；3. 精确嵌入计数动态规划：用于计算这些结构化分布的互信息I；4. 混合神经-精确方法：结合变分评论家(InfoNCE、NWJ、DV/MINE、SMILE)作为内部搜索目标，恢复N≤8的认证ORD权重；5. ORD连续迁移：从精确小N解学习归一化游程计数分布，重采样到目标长度(最高N=512)；6. 利用Fertonani-Duman长度熵不等式将精确ORD速率转换为C(d)的有效下界。

Result: 1. 直接分数函数学习的ORD权重在N≥32时崩溃为平坦游程长度分布(RLD)；2. 迁移ORD在中等删除概率下始终优于RLD，在d=0.1时增益约在N=128-192处消失；3. 提供了N≤8的认证ORD权重；4. 通过嵌套蒙特卡洛评估大N输入作为诊断工具（不声称是容量下界）；5. 固定N=100的样本预算研究量化了嵌套MC偏差；6. 所有主要报告的速率均为I(X^N;Y)/N的值。

Conclusion: 该工作系统研究了二进制删除信道的有限块长容量问题，通过轨道对称性简化和ORD模型大幅降低了优化复杂度。提出的ORD连续迁移方法成功将小规模精确解扩展到大规模场景(N=512)，在中等删除概率下优于传统RLD方法。研究同时强调了严格认证的重要性——区分了经精确计算的速率与仅作为诊断的嵌套蒙特卡洛估计，为有限块长信道容量研究提供了可靠的方法论框架。

Abstract: For a binary deletion channel operating on fixed-length inputs, the relevant figure of merit is the finite-blocklength capacity $C_N(d)=\max_{p(x^N)}\mathrm{I}(X^N;Y)$, not only the infinite-blocklength limit $C(d)$. We show that an optimal input may be chosen constant on complement and permutation-equivalence orbits, reducing the optimization to one weight per orbit, and introduce the optimized run distribution (ORD), an $N$-parameter run-count model that coincides with $C_N(d)$ for $N\le 3$ and is optimal among all run-count-constant inputs. An exact embedding-count dynamic program evaluates $\mathrm{I}$ for these structured laws. A hybrid neural--exact procedure recovers certified ORD weights for $N\le 8$; variational critics (InfoNCE, NWJ, DV/MINE, SMILE) are used only as inner search objectives. Direct score-function learning of ORD weights collapses toward a flat run-length distribution (RLD) for $N\ge 32$. We therefore introduce ORD continuum transfer: a normalized run-count profile learned from exact small-$N$ ORD solutions is resampled at target lengths up to $N=512$. Transferred ORD consistently outperforms RLD at moderate deletion probabilities; at $d=0.1$ the gain vanishes by roughly $N=128$--$192$. Exact ORD rates converted by Fertonani--Duman's length-entropy inequality are valid lower bounds on $C(d)$; nested-Monte-Carlo evaluations of large-$N$ inputs are reported as diagnostics and are not claimed as capacity lower bounds. A fixed-$N=100$ sample-budget study quantifies nested-MC bias. All primary reported rates are values of $\mathrm{I}(X^N;Y)/N$.

</details>


### [104] [Improved Lower Bounds on the Capacity of the Binary Deletion Channel via a Learning Approach to Run-Length Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24908)
*Hassan Khodaiemehr,Chen Feng,Tolga M. Duman*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: This paper improves lower bounds on the binary deletion channel capacity by optimizing the run-length distribution via gradient-based learning rather than restricting to parametric families, achieving the best known bounds at all tested deletion probabilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The capacity C(d) of the i.i.d. binary deletion channel exists but has no closed form. Classical constructive lower bounds from i.i.d. run-length coding have been limited to one- or two-parameter families (geometric, Markov, or Morse-type), which may not achieve the optimal performance.

Method: 1) Treat the run-length law P as a free distribution and optimize it by learning instead of restricting to parametric families; 2) Reduce the Drinea-Mitzenmacher functional to a bilinear form in P and prove that finite-support truncation is one-sided, ensuring computed values remain valid lower bounds; 3) Extend the Venkataramanan et al. reductions from geometric runs to arbitrary finite-support laws, including a residual-run HMM for output-bit entropy; 4) Use softmax gradient ascent to search P; 5) Evaluate bounds without Monte Carlo and without finite length-entropy penalty.

Result: The envelope of the two optimized bounds exceeds Gallager's 1-h(d) (for d<1/2) and the tabulated bounds of Drinea-Mitzenmacher, Venkataramanan et al., and Rubinstein-Con at every tested d. Representative lower bounds: C(d)≥0.92212, 0.72939, 0.56486, 0.35127, 0.22616, 0.10414, 0.02891, 0.01322 at d=0.01, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 0.90. The largest absolute gain over previous record is 3.7×10^-3 bits (at d=0.30); the largest relative gain is 6.8% (at d=0.90). For d≤0.45 the envelope is the free-P Venkataramanan functional; from d=0.50 it is the learned Drinea-Mitzenmacher law. At large d, the optimizer finds sparse run-length combs that parametric families cannot represent. Compared to concurrent work by Papailiopoulos, the envelope remains larger at high d.

Conclusion: By treating the run-length law as a free distribution and optimizing via learning, the same infinite-blocklength functionals become strictly stronger than previous parametric approaches. The method provides fresh one-sided lower bounds that surpass classical results across all tested deletion probabilities, with particularly notable improvements at high deletion rates where non-parametric sparse structures emerge.

Abstract: The capacity $C(d)$ of the i.i.d. binary deletion channel exists by Dobrushin's information-stability theorem, but no closed form is known. Classical constructive lower bounds from i.i.d. run-length coding have been evaluated only for one- or two-parameter families (geometric, Markov, or Morse-type). We show that the same infinite-blocklength functionals become strictly stronger when the run-length law $P$ is treated as a free distribution and optimized by learning. We reduce the Drinea--Mitzenmacher functional to a bilinear form in $P$ and prove that finite-support truncation is one-sided, so computed values remain valid lower bounds. We extend the Venkataramanan et al. reductions from geometric runs to arbitrary finite-support laws, including a residual-run HMM for output-bit entropy. Softmax gradient ascent searches $P$; every reported number is a fresh one-sided evaluation of the formula, with no Monte Carlo and no finite length-entropy penalty. The envelope of the two optimized bounds exceeds Gallager's $1-h(d)$ (for $d<1/2$) and the tabulated bounds of Drinea--Mitzenmacher, Venkataramanan et al., and Rubinstein--Con at every tested $d$. Representative values: $C(d)\ge 0.92212$, $0.72939$, $0.56486$, $0.35127$, $0.22616$, $0.10414$, $0.02891$, $0.01322$ at $d=0.01$, $0.05$, $0.10$, $0.20$, $0.30$, $0.50$, $0.80$, $0.90$. The largest absolute gain over that record is $3.7\times 10^{-3}$ bits (at $d=0.30$); the largest relative gain is $6.8\%$ (at $d=0.90$). For $d\le 0.45$ the envelope is the free-$P$ Venkataramanan functional; from $d=0.50$ it is the learned Drinea--Mitzenmacher law. At large $d$ the optimizer finds sparse run-length combs that parametric families cannot represent. A concurrent enclosure of Papailiopoulos is stronger on much of $[0,1]$, but our envelope remains larger at high $d$ (e.g. $0.02891$ vs $0.02884$ at $d=0.80$; $0.01322$ vs $0.01293$ at $d=0.90$).

</details>


### [105] [Feedback Coding Enables Inference-Time Covert Agentic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24994)
*Sidong Guo,Sajani Vithana,Atefeh Gilani,Lalitha Sankar,Oliver Kosut,Flavio P. Calmon*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出BAM，一种基于反馈编码的黑盒大语言模型隐写方案，将隐写重新建模为具有因果无噪声反馈的序列通信问题，通过结合后验匹配与解码确认机制，在约50个token内实现8位载荷0-0.1%的错误率，显著优于现有黑盒基线方法（10-17%错误率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于自动化数字交互，用户可能利用LLM生成的文本作为掩护进行隐蔽通信。现有LLM隐写技术主要为白盒方案，要求收发双方共享模型权重和提示等覆盖文本统计信息。黑盒方案虽消除了该要求，但当前方法依赖固定长度开环水印技术，在变长token生成场景下解码错误率高。因此，需要一种无需共享模型内部信息、且能适应变长生成的黑盒隐写方案。

Method: 作者将黑盒LLM隐写重新建模为具有因果无噪声反馈的序列通信问题：每个生成的token对双方可见并可指导后续嵌入。基于此视角，提出Burnashev自适应后验匹配（BAM）方案，该反馈编码方案结合后验匹配（posterior matching）与解码确认阶段（decode-and-confirm phase）。设计灵感来源于经典信息论反馈编码原理，安全性通过密码学归约证明建立。在此基础上，构建端到端通信协议并在多种对话场景中验证。

Result: 在三个开源权重语言模型上的实验表明：BAM在约50个token长度内，对8位载荷实现0-0.1%的经验消息错误率（1000次试验），而同等长度下最强黑盒基线方法的错误率为10-17%。此外，基于所提隐写算法构建的端到端通信协议在多种对话设置中实现了高通信速率。

Conclusion: BAM通过反馈编码视角有效解决了黑盒LLM隐写在变长token生成中的高错误率问题，在极短文本长度下实现了接近无误的隐蔽通信，为实际对话场景中的安全隐蔽通信提供了可行方案。该工作将经典信息论反馈编码原理与现代LLM应用相结合，展示了理论框架指导实际系统设计的潜力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate digital interactions, users can leverage LLM-generated text as cover for covert communication within seemingly benign conversations. Existing LLM steganography, however, is predominantly white-box, requiring the sender and receiver to share the cover statistics, typically through access to the model weights and prompt. Black-box schemes remove this requirement by allowing the receiver to operate solely on the generated text, but current approaches rely on fixed-length, open-loop watermarking techniques that suffer from high decoding error rates under variable-length token generation. We recast black-box LLM steganography as a sequential communication problem with causal, noiseless feedback: every generated token is observed by both parties and can guide subsequent embedding. Based on this perspective, we introduce \textbf{B}urnashev \textbf{A}daptive Posterior \textbf{M}atching (BAM), a feedback-coding scheme that combines posterior matching with a decode-and-confirm phase. The design is inspired by classical information-theoretic feedback-coding principles, while its security is established through a cryptographic reduction proof. Across three open-weight language models, we demonstrate that BAM attains 0-0.1\% empirical message error on an 8-bit payload in around 50 tokens, across 1000 trials, versus 10-17\% for the strongest black-box baseline at comparable length. Building on the proposed steganography algorithm, we demonstrate the feasibility of an end-to-end communication protocol that achieves high communication rates across multiple conversational settings.

</details>
